1	"""RSFT (Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning) 训练脚本。
2	
3	从 SFT warmup checkpoint 继续训练，使用经 CED 打分筛选的高质量生成数据。
4	流程: base model → merge SFT adapter → 新 QLoRA → 在 RSFT 数据上训练。
5	
6	输入: JSONL，每行 {"doc_id": ..., "input": ..., "output": ..., "ced_score": ..., ...}
7	输出: QLoRA adapter checkpoint
8	依赖: transformers, trl, peft, bitsandbytes, datasets
9	
10	单卡:   python -m freige.training.rsft_trainer --rsft_data_path data.jsonl --output_dir ./rsft_output
11	多卡:   accelerate launch --config_file configs/accelerate_4gpu.yaml \
12	            -m freige.training.rsft_trainer --rsft_data_path data.jsonl --output_dir ./rsft_output
13	"""
14	
15	import argparse
16	import json
17	import logging
18	import os
19	
20	import torch
21	from datasets import Dataset
22	from peft import LoraConfig, PeftModel, TaskType, get_peft_model
23	from torch.utils.data import WeightedRandomSampler
24	from transformers import (
25	    AutoModelForCausalLM,
26	    AutoTokenizer,
27	    BitsAndBytesConfig,
28	    TrainerCallback,
29	)
30	from trl import SFTConfig, SFTTrainer
31	
32	from freige.training.sft_trainer import SYSTEM_PROMPT
33	
34	logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
35	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
36	
37	DEFAULT_BASE_MODEL = "/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B"
38	DEFAULT_SFT_ADAPTER = "/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output"
39	
40	
41	def load_rsft_data(data_path: str, tokenizer, max_length: int = 2048):
42	    """加载 CED 筛选后的 RSFT 数据，格式化为 chat template 文本。
43	
44	    每行 JSONL 的 input 字段作为 user message，output 字段作为 assistant response，
45	    经 apply_chat_template 拼接后用于 SFT 训练。超过 max_length 的样本被跳过
46	    而非截断，避免训练在不完整 JSON 输出上。
47	
48	    Returns:
49	        (Dataset, list[float]): 训练数据集和对应的 CED scores
50	    """
51	    samples = []
52	    with open(data_path) as f:
53	        for line in f:
54	            if line.strip():
55	                samples.append(json.loads(line))
56	
57	    logger.info("Loaded %d RSFT samples from %s", len(samples), data_path)
58	
59	    formatted = []
60	    ced_scores = []
61	    skipped = 0
62	    for s in samples:
63	        messages = [
64	            {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT},
65	            {"role": "user", "content": s["input"]},
66	            {"role": "assistant", "content": s["output"]},
67	        ]
68	        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
69	            messages,
70	            tokenize=False,
71	            add_generation_prompt=False,
72	            enable_thinking=False,
73	        )
74	        token_len = len(tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False))
75	        if token_len > max_length:
76	            skipped += 1
77	            continue
78	        formatted.append({"text": text})
79	        ced_scores.append(s.get("ced_score", 1.0))
80	
81	    if skipped:
82	        logger.warning("Skipped %d samples exceeding max_length=%d", skipped, max_length)
83	
84	    if ced_scores:
85	        logger.info(
86	            "Prepared %d RSFT samples (CED scores: min=%.3f, max=%.3f, mean=%.3f)",
87	            len(formatted), min(ced_scores), max(ced_scores),
88	            sum(ced_scores) / len(ced_scores),
89	        )
90	    return Dataset.from_list(formatted), ced_scores
91	
92	
93	def prepare_eval_data(data_dir, tokenizer, max_docs=50):
94	    """Load DocRED dev set for task-level evaluation during training."""
95	    from freige.data.docred_processor import DocREDProcessor
96	    from freige.eval.evaluator import gold_from_docred
97	
98	    processor = DocREDProcessor(data_dir=data_dir)
99	    samples = processor.process("dev")
100	    groups = processor.group_by_document(samples)
101	
102	    eval_items = []
103	    for doc_id, doc_samples in list(groups.items())[:max_docs]:
104	        sft_sample = processor.format_sft_sample(doc_samples)
105	        messages = [
106	            {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT},
107	            {"role": "user", "content": f"{sft_sample['instruction']}\n\n{sft_sample['input']}"},
108	        ]
109	        prompt = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
110	            messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False,
111	        )
112	        eval_items.append({"doc_id": doc_id, "prompt": prompt})
113	
114	    dev_path = os.path.join(data_dir, "dev.json")
115	    with open(dev_path) as f:
116	        dev_data = json.load(f)
117	    gold = gold_from_docred(dev_data)
118	
119	    # Also build formatted eval dataset for loss-based eval
120	    eval_formatted = []
121	    for doc_id, doc_samples in groups.items():
122	        sft_sample = processor.format_sft_sample(doc_samples)
123	        messages = [
124	            {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT},
125	            {"role": "user", "content": f"{sft_sample['instruction']}\n\n{sft_sample['input']}"},
126	            {"role": "assistant", "content": sft_sample["output"]},
127	        ]
128	        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
129	            messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=False, enable_thinking=False,
130	        )
131	        eval_formatted.append({"text": text})
132	    eval_dataset = Dataset.from_list(eval_formatted)
133	
134	    return eval_items, gold, eval_dataset
135	
136	
137	class TaskEvalCallback(TrainerCallback):
138	    """End-of-epoch task eval: generate on dev subset → compute F1 / Evi-F1 / EDCR."""
139	
140	    def __init__(self, tokenizer, eval_items, gold, train_file, max_new_tokens=1024):
141	        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
142	        self.eval_items = eval_items
143	        self.gold = gold
144	        self.train_file = train_file
145	        self.max_new_tokens = max_new_tokens
146	        self._evaluator = None
147	
148	    @property
149	    def evaluator(self):
150	        if self._evaluator is None:
151	            from freige.eval.evaluator import DocREDEvaluator
152	            if self.train_file and os.path.exists(self.train_file):
153	                self._evaluator = DocREDEvaluator.from_train_file(self.train_file)
154	            else:
155	                self._evaluator = DocREDEvaluator()
156	        return self._evaluator
157	
158	    def on_epoch_end(self, args, state, control, model=None, **kwargs):
159	        if not state.is_world_process_zero:
160	            return
161	        self._run_task_eval(model, state)
162	
163	    def _run_task_eval(self, model, state):
164	        from freige.eval.evaluator import parse_model_output
165	
166	        model.eval()
167	        model.config.use_cache = True
168	        if hasattr(model, "gradient_checkpointing_disable"):
169	            model.gradient_checkpointing_disable()
170	
171	        all_predictions = []
172	        for item in self.eval_items:
173	            inputs = self.tokenizer(
174	                item["prompt"], return_tensors="pt", truncation=True, max_length=4096,
175	            ).to(model.device)
176	            with torch.no_grad():
177	                outputs = model.generate(
178	                    **inputs,
179	                    max_new_tokens=self.max_new_tokens,
180	                    do_sample=False,
181	                    pad_token_id=self.tokenizer.pad_token_id,
182	                )
183	            generated = outputs[0][inputs["input_ids"].shape[1]:]
184	            text = self.tokenizer.decode(generated, skip_special_tokens=True)
185	            triples, _ = parse_model_output(text)
186	            for t in triples:
187	                t["doc_id"] = item["doc_id"]
188	            all_predictions.extend(triples)
189	
190	        metrics = self.evaluator.evaluate(all_predictions, self.gold)
191	
192	        log_metrics = {
193	            "eval/rel_f1": metrics["f1"],
194	            "eval/ign_f1": metrics["ign_f1"],
195	            "eval/evi_f1": metrics["evi_f1"],
196	            "eval/evi_precision": metrics["evi_precision"],
197	            "eval/evi_recall": metrics["evi_recall"],
198	            "eval/edcr": metrics.get("edcr", 0),
199	            "eval/n_predictions": len(all_predictions),
200	        }
201	        try:
202	            import wandb
203	            if wandb.run is not None:
204	                wandb.log(log_metrics, step=state.global_step)
205	        except ImportError:
206	            pass
207	
208	        logger.info(
209	            "Task eval @ step %d: F1=%.4f, Ign-F1=%.4f, Evi-F1=%.4f, EDCR=%.4f (%d preds)",
210	            state.global_step, metrics["f1"], metrics["ign_f1"],
211	            metrics["evi_f1"], metrics.get("edcr", 0), len(all_predictions),
212	        )
213	
214	        model.config.use_cache = False
215	        if hasattr(model, "gradient_checkpointing_enable"):
216	            model.gradient_checkpointing_enable()
217	        model.train()
218	
219	
220	class WeightedSFTTrainer(SFTTrainer):
221	    """SFTTrainer with CED score-weighted sampling.
222	
223	    高 CED score 的样本被更频繁采样。使用 WeightedRandomSampler 实现，
224	    适用于单机训练。多机 DDP 场景下 Accelerate 会替换 sampler，
225	    此时 weighted_sampling 不生效，需改用数据集层面的过采样。
226	    """
227	
228	    def __init__(self, *args, sample_weights=None, **kwargs):
229	        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
230	        self.sample_weights = sample_weights
231	
232	    def _get_train_sampler(self, dataset=None):
233	        if self.sample_weights is not None:
234	            ds = dataset if dataset is not None else self.train_dataset
235	            return WeightedRandomSampler(
236	                weights=self.sample_weights,
237	                num_samples=len(ds),
238	                replacement=True,
239	            )
240	        return super()._get_train_sampler(dataset)
241	
242	
243	def main():
244	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="FREIGE RSFT Training")
245	    parser.add_argument("--base_model", type=str, default=DEFAULT_BASE_MODEL)
246	    parser.add_argument("--sft_adapter", type=str, default=DEFAULT_SFT_ADAPTER,
247	                        help="SFT warmup adapter 路径 (merge 后训练新 LoRA)")
248	    parser.add_argument("--rsft_data_path", type=str, required=True,
249	                        help="CED 筛选后的 RSFT 训练数据 (JSONL)")
250	    parser.add_argument("--output_dir", type=str, required=True)
251	    parser.add_argument("--learning_rate", type=float, default=2e-5)
252	    parser.add_argument("--num_epochs", type=int, default=3)
253	    parser.add_argument("--per_device_batch_size", type=int, default=4)
254	    parser.add_argument("--gradient_accumulation_steps", type=int, default=4)
255	    parser.add_argument("--warmup_steps", type=int, default=100)
256	    parser.add_argument("--save_steps", type=int, default=50)
257	    parser.add_argument("--save_total_limit", type=int, default=5)
258	    parser.add_argument("--max_length", type=int, default=2048)
259	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
260	    parser.add_argument("--wandb_project", type=str, default="freige-rsft")
261	    parser.add_argument("--wandb_run_name", type=str, default=None)
262	    parser.add_argument("--weighted_sampling", action="store_true",
263	                        help="按 CED score 加权采样（高分样本更频繁）")
264	    parser.add_argument("--quantize", action="store_true",
265	                        help="启用 4-bit QLoRA 量化")
266	    parser.add_argument("--lora_rank", type=int, default=64)
267	    parser.add_argument("--lora_alpha", type=int, default=128)
268	    parser.add_argument("--lora_dropout", type=float, default=0.05)
269	    parser.add_argument("--bf16", action="store_true", default=True)
270	    parser.add_argument("--deepspeed", type=str, default=None,
271	                        help="DeepSpeed 配置文件路径")
272	    parser.add_argument("--eval_data_dir", type=str, default=None,
273	                        help="DocRED data dir (contains dev.json) for task-level eval")
274	    parser.add_argument("--eval_max_docs", type=int, default=50,
275	                        help="Max dev docs for task-level eval during training")
276	    parser.add_argument("--max_new_tokens", type=int, default=1024)
277	    parser.add_argument("--max_steps", type=int, default=-1,
278	                        help="Override num_epochs: stop after N steps (-1 = use num_epochs)")
279	    args = parser.parse_args()
280	
281	    # W&B
282	    if args.wandb_project and "WANDB_API_KEY" in os.environ:
283	        os.environ["WANDB_PROJECT"] = args.wandb_project
284	        if args.wandb_run_name:
285	            os.environ["WANDB_NAME"] = args.wandb_run_name
286	
287	    # Tokenizer
288	    logger.info("Loading tokenizer: %s", args.base_model)
289	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
290	        args.base_model, trust_remote_code=True, padding_side="right",
291	    )
292	    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
293	        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
294	
295	    # Data
296	    logger.info("Loading RSFT data: %s", args.rsft_data_path)
297	    train_dataset, ced_scores = load_rsft_data(
298	        args.rsft_data_path, tokenizer, args.max_length,
299	    )
300	
301	    # Model: base → merge SFT adapter → new LoRA
302	    logger.info("Loading base model: %s", args.base_model)
303	    local_rank = int(os.environ.get("LOCAL_RANK", 0))
304	    model_kwargs = dict(
305	        trust_remote_code=True,
306	        attn_implementation="sdpa",
307	        device_map={"": local_rank},
308	    )
309	    if args.quantize:
310	        logger.info("Using 4-bit QLoRA quantization")
311	        model_kwargs["quantization_config"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(
312	            load_in_4bit=True,
313	            bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
314	            bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
315	            bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
316	        )
317	    else:
318	        model_kwargs["torch_dtype"] = torch.bfloat16 if args.bf16 else torch.float16
319	
320	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(args.base_model, **model_kwargs)
321	
322	    if args.sft_adapter:
323	        logger.info("Merging SFT adapter: %s", args.sft_adapter)
324	        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.sft_adapter)
325	        model = model.merge_and_unload()
326	
327	    model.config.use_cache = False
328	    model.gradient_checkpointing_enable()
329	
330	    # Fresh LoRA for RSFT (与 SFT warmup 相同结构)
331	    lora_config = LoraConfig(
332	        task_type=TaskType.CAUSAL_LM,
333	        r=args.lora_rank,
334	        lora_alpha=args.lora_alpha,
335	        lora_dropout=args.lora_dropout,
336	        target_modules=["q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj",
337	                         "gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj"],
338	    )
339	    model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)
340	    model.print_trainable_parameters()
341	
342	    # Eval data
343	    eval_dataset = None
344	    callbacks = []
345	    if args.eval_data_dir:
346	        logger.info("Loading eval data from %s (max %d docs for task eval)",
347	                     args.eval_data_dir, args.eval_max_docs)
348	        eval_items, gold, eval_dataset = prepare_eval_data(
349	            args.eval_data_dir, tokenizer, args.eval_max_docs,
350	        )
351	        train_file = os.path.join(args.eval_data_dir, "train_annotated.json")
352	        callbacks.append(TaskEvalCallback(
353	            tokenizer=tokenizer,
354	            eval_items=eval_items,
355	            gold=gold,
356	            train_file=train_file,
357	            max_new_tokens=args.max_new_tokens,
358	        ))
359	        logger.info("Task eval enabled: %d docs, %d gold triples",
360	                     len(eval_items), len(gold))
361	
362	    # Training config
363	    training_args = SFTConfig(
364	        output_dir=args.output_dir,
365	        num_train_epochs=args.num_epochs,
366	        per_device_train_batch_size=args.per_device_batch_size,
367	        per_device_eval_batch_size=1,
368	        gradient_accumulation_steps=args.gradient_accumulation_steps,
369	        learning_rate=args.learning_rate,
370	        warmup_steps=args.warmup_steps,
371	        bf16=args.bf16,
372	        logging_steps=10,
373	        eval_strategy="epoch" if eval_dataset is not None else "no",
374	        save_strategy="steps",
375	        save_steps=args.save_steps,
376	        save_total_limit=args.save_total_limit,
377	        seed=args.seed,
378	        max_length=args.max_length,
379	        dataset_text_field="text",
380	        report_to="wandb" if "WANDB_API_KEY" in os.environ else "none",
381	        gradient_checkpointing=True,
382	        gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False},
383	        deepspeed=args.deepspeed,
384	        max_steps=args.max_steps,
385	    )
386	
387	    sample_weights = ced_scores if args.weighted_sampling else None
388	
389	    trainer = WeightedSFTTrainer(
390	        model=model,
391	        args=training_args,
392	        train_dataset=train_dataset,
393	        eval_dataset=eval_dataset,
394	        processing_class=tokenizer,
395	        sample_weights=sample_weights,
396	        callbacks=callbacks,
397	    )
398	
399	    logger.info(
400	        "Starting RSFT training (%d samples, weighted=%s)...",
401	        len(train_dataset), args.weighted_sampling,
402	    )
403	    trainer.train()
404	
405	    logger.info("Saving model to %s", args.output_dir)
406	    trainer.save_model(args.output_dir)
407	    tokenizer.save_pretrained(args.output_dir)
408	    logger.info("RSFT training complete.")

1	"""RSFT Step 1: 批量生成候选输出。
2	
3	用 SFT warmup 模型对每个训练样本生成 N 个候选输出 (temperature sampling)，
4	保存为 JSONL 供后续 CED 打分筛选。
5	
6	输入:
7	  - base model (Qwen3-4B) + QLoRA adapter (SFT checkpoint)
8	  - DocRED train_annotated.json
9	
10	输出 (保存到 --output_dir):
11	  - generations.jsonl: 每行一个生成结果
12	    {"doc_id", "generation_idx", "prompt", "output", "raw_text", "parsed_triples", "format_ok"}
13	  - progress.json: 已完成的 doc_id 列表（用于断点续传）
14	
15	依赖: transformers, peft, bitsandbytes, torch, tqdm
16	
17	用法:
18	  python -m freige.training.rsft_generate \
19	      --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B \
20	      --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output \
21	      --data_path /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred \
22	      --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \
23	      --num_generations 8 --temperature 0.7 --batch_size 4
24	"""
25	
26	import argparse
27	import json
28	import logging
29	import os
30	from pathlib import Path
31	
32	import torch
33	from peft import PeftModel
34	from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer, BitsAndBytesConfig
35	from tqdm import tqdm
36	
37	from freige.data.docred_processor import DocREDProcessor
38	from freige.eval.evaluator import parse_model_output
39	
40	logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
41	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
42	
43	SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
44	    "You are an information extraction model. Given a document with numbered sentences "
45	    "and a list of entities, extract all relation triples. For each triple, provide the "
46	    "head entity, relation type, tail entity, and the sentence IDs that serve as evidence. "
47	    "Output as a JSON list."
48	)
49	
50	
51	def load_model_and_tokenizer(base_model, sft_adapter=None, quantize=True):
52	    """加载 base model + 可选 QLoRA adapter，merge 后返回。"""
53	    logger.info("Loading tokenizer: %s", base_model)
54	    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(
55	        base_model, trust_remote_code=True, padding_side="left",
56	    )
57	    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
58	        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
59	
60	    logger.info("Loading model: %s", base_model)
61	    model_kwargs = dict(trust_remote_code=True, attn_implementation="sdpa", device_map="auto")
62	    if quantize:
63	        model_kwargs["quantization_config"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(
64	            load_in_4bit=True,
65	            bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
66	            bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
67	            bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
68	        )
69	    else:
70	        model_kwargs["torch_dtype"] = torch.bfloat16
71	
72	    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(base_model, **model_kwargs)
73	
74	    if sft_adapter:
75	        logger.info("Loading & merging SFT adapter: %s", sft_adapter)
76	        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, sft_adapter)
77	        model = model.merge_and_unload()
78	
79	    model.eval()
80	    return model, tokenizer
81	
82	
83	def prepare_generation_data(data_dir, split, tokenizer):
84	    """加载 DocRED 数据，构建每个文档的 prompt 和 gold 信息。
85	
86	    Returns:
87	        list of dict: 每个文档一条，含 doc_id, prompt, gold_triples, sents
88	    """
89	    processor = DocREDProcessor(data_dir=data_dir)
90	    samples = processor.process(split)
91	    groups = processor.group_by_document(samples)
92	
93	    items = []
94	    for doc_id, doc_samples in groups.items():
95	        sft_sample = processor.format_sft_sample(doc_samples)
96	        messages = [
97	            {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT},
98	            {"role": "user", "content": f"{sft_sample['instruction']}\n\n{sft_sample['input']}"},
99	        ]
100	        prompt = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
101	            messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False,
102	        )
103	
104	        gold_triples = []
105	        for s in doc_samples:
106	            gold_triples.append({
107	                "head": s.head.name,
108	                "relation": s.relation_name,
109	                "tail": s.tail.name,
110	                "evidence": s.evidence_sent_ids,
111	                "hard_negative_sent_ids": s.hard_negative_sent_ids,
112	            })
113	
114	        ref = doc_samples[0]
115	        items.append({
116	            "doc_id": doc_id,
117	            "prompt": prompt,
118	            "gold_triples": gold_triples,
119	            "sents": ref.sents,
120	            "sft_output": sft_sample["output"],
121	        })
122	
123	    logger.info("Prepared %d documents for generation", len(items))
124	    return items
125	
126	
127	def load_progress(output_dir):
128	    """加载已完成的 doc_id 集合（断点续传）。"""
129	    progress_path = Path(output_dir) / "progress.json"
130	    if progress_path.exists():
131	        with open(progress_path) as f:
132	            data = json.load(f)
133	        completed = set(data.get("completed_doc_ids", []))
134	        logger.info("Resuming: %d documents already completed", len(completed))
135	        return completed
136	    return set()
137	
138	
139	def save_progress(output_dir, completed_doc_ids):
140	    """保存已完成 doc_id 列表。"""
141	    progress_path = Path(output_dir) / "progress.json"
142	    with open(progress_path, "w") as f:
143	        json.dump({"completed_doc_ids": sorted(completed_doc_ids)}, f)
144	
145	
146	@torch.no_grad()
147	def generate_batch(model, tokenizer, prompts, num_generations, temperature, max_new_tokens):
148	    """对一批 prompt 各生成 num_generations 个输出。
149	
150	    Returns:
151	        list[list[str]]: outer = prompts, inner = num_generations 个输出文本
152	    """
153	    # 将每个 prompt 重复 num_generations 次，一次性生成
154	    expanded_prompts = []
155	    for p in prompts:
156	        expanded_prompts.extend([p] * num_generations)
157	
158	    inputs = tokenizer(
159	        expanded_prompts, return_tensors="pt", padding=True, truncation=True,
160	        max_length=4096,
161	    ).to(model.device)
162	
163	    generate_kwargs = dict(
164	        max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
165	        do_sample=True,
166	        temperature=temperature,
167	        top_p=0.9,
168	        pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id,
169	    )
170	
171	    outputs = model.generate(**inputs, **generate_kwargs)
172	
173	    # 截取新生成的 token
174	    input_len = inputs["input_ids"].shape[1]
175	    generated_ids = outputs[:, input_len:]
176	    texts = tokenizer.batch_decode(generated_ids, skip_special_tokens=True)
177	
178	    # 按 prompt 分组
179	    results = []
180	    for i in range(len(prompts)):
181	        start = i * num_generations
182	        end = start + num_generations
183	        results.append(texts[start:end])
184	
185	    return results
186	
187	
188	def main():
189	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="RSFT Step 1: Batch Generation")
190	    parser.add_argument("--base_model", type=str, default="/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B",
191	                        help="Base model 路径")
192	    parser.add_argument("--sft_adapter", type=str, default="/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output",
193	                        help="SFT QLoRA adapter 路径")
194	    parser.add_argument("--data_path", type=str, default=None,
195	                        help="DocRED 数据目录")
196	    parser.add_argument("--split", type=str, default="train",
197	                        help="数据集 split (default: train)")
198	    parser.add_argument("--output_dir", type=str, default="/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations",
199	                        help="生成结果输出目录")
200	    parser.add_argument("--num_generations", type=int, default=8,
201	                        help="每个样本生成的候选数 (default: 8)")
202	    parser.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=0.7,
203	                        help="采样温度 (default: 0.7)")
204	    parser.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=2,
205	                        help="每批处理的文档数 (default: 2)")
206	    parser.add_argument("--max_new_tokens", type=int, default=1024,
207	                        help="最大生成 token 数 (default: 1024)")
208	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
209	    parser.add_argument("--quantize", action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
210	                        default=False, help="4-bit 量化 (default: off, bf16 sufficient for 4B model)")
211	    args = parser.parse_args()
212	
213	    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
214	    if torch.cuda.is_available():
215	        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(args.seed)
216	
217	    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)
218	    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
219	    output_file = output_dir / "generations.jsonl"
220	
221	    model, tokenizer = load_model_and_tokenizer(
222	        args.base_model, args.sft_adapter, quantize=args.quantize,
223	    )
224	
225	    items = prepare_generation_data(args.data_path, args.split, tokenizer)
226	
227	    completed = load_progress(args.output_dir)
228	    pending = [item for item in items if item["doc_id"] not in completed]
229	    logger.info("Total: %d, completed: %d, pending: %d", len(items), len(completed), len(pending))
230	
231	    if not pending:
232	        logger.info("All documents already generated. Done.")
233	        return
234	
235	    # 追加写模式
236	    with open(output_file, "a") as fout:
237	        for batch_start in tqdm(range(0, len(pending), args.batch_size), desc="Generating"):
238	            batch = pending[batch_start:batch_start + args.batch_size]
239	            prompts = [item["prompt"] for item in batch]
240	
241	            try:
242	                batch_outputs = generate_batch(
243	                    model, tokenizer, prompts,
244	                    args.num_generations, args.temperature, args.max_new_tokens,
245	                )
246	            except torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError:
247	                logger.warning("OOM on batch of %d, falling back to one-by-one", len(batch))
248	                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
249	                batch_outputs = []
250	                for p in prompts:
251	                    out = generate_batch(
252	                        model, tokenizer, [p],
253	                        args.num_generations, args.temperature, args.max_new_tokens,
254	                    )
255	                    batch_outputs.append(out[0])
256	
257	            for item, generations in zip(batch, batch_outputs):
258	                for gen_idx, raw_text in enumerate(generations):
259	                    parsed_triples, format_ok = parse_model_output(raw_text)
260	                    record = {
261	                        "doc_id": item["doc_id"],
262	                        "generation_idx": gen_idx,
263	                        "prompt": item["prompt"],
264	                        "output": raw_text,
265	                        "raw_text": raw_text,
266	                        "parsed_triples": parsed_triples,
267	                        "format_ok": format_ok,
268	                        "gold_triples": item["gold_triples"],
269	                        "sents": item["sents"],
270	                    }
271	                    fout.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
272	
273	                completed.add(item["doc_id"])
274	
275	            fout.flush()
276	            save_progress(args.output_dir, completed)
277	
278	    logger.info("Generation complete. %d documents, %d total outputs → %s",
279	                len(items), len(items) * args.num_generations, output_file)
280	
281	    # 统计
282	    n_format_ok = 0
283	    n_total = 0
284	    with open(output_file) as f:
285	        for line in f:
286	            rec = json.loads(line)
287	            n_total += 1
288	            if rec.get("format_ok"):
289	                n_format_ok += 1
290	
291	    logger.info("Format compliance: %d/%d (%.1f%%)", n_format_ok, n_total,
292	                100 * n_format_ok / n_total if n_total else 0)
293	
294	
295	if __name__ == "__main__":
296	    main()
297

111	class DocREDProcessor:
112	    """DocRED 数据加载与预处理。
113	
114	    Args:
115	        data_dir: DocRED JSON 文件目录。为 None 时从 HuggingFace 加载。
116	    """
117	
118	    def __init__(self, data_dir: Optional[str] = None):
119	        self.data_dir = Path(data_dir) if data_dir else None
120	
121	    def load_split(self, split: str) -> list[dict]:
122	        """加载指定 split 的原始 JSON 数据。"""
123	        if self.data_dir is not None:
124	            fname = SPLIT_FILENAMES.get(split, f"{split}.json")
125	            path = self.data_dir / fname
126	            if not path.exists():
127	                raise FileNotFoundError(f"DocRED file not found: {path}")
128	            with open(path) as f:
129	                data = json.load(f)
130	            logger.info("Loaded %d documents from %s", len(data), path)
131	            return data
132	
133	        from datasets import load_dataset
134	        ds = load_dataset("thunlp/docred", split=split, trust_remote_code=True)
135	        data = [row for row in ds]
136	        logger.info("Loaded %d documents from HuggingFace (split=%s)", len(data), split)
137	        return data
138	
139	    def _tokens_to_text(self, tokens: list[str]) -> str:
140	        """将 token 列表拼接为句子文本，处理标点前空格。"""
141	        if not tokens:
142	            return ""
143	        result = tokens[0]
144	        no_space_before = {".", ",", "!", "?", ";", ":", "'", ")", "]", "}",
145	                           "'s", "'t", "'re", "'ve", "'ll", "'d", "'m", "n't"}
146	        for tok in tokens[1:]:
147	            if tok in no_space_before or tok.startswith("'"):
148	                result += tok
149	            else:
150	                result += " " + tok
151	        return result
152	
153	    def _parse_document(self, doc: dict) -> tuple[str, list[str], list[Entity]]:
154	        """解析单个文档，返回 (全文, 句子列表, 实体列表)。"""
155	        sents_tokens = doc["sents"]
156	        sents = [self._tokens_to_text(s) for s in sents_tokens]
157	        document = " ".join(sents)
158	
159	        entities = []
160	        for eidx, entity_mentions in enumerate(doc["vertexSet"]):
161	            mentions = []
162	            for m in entity_mentions:
163	                mentions.append(EntityMention(
164	                    name=m["name"],
165	                    sent_id=m["sent_id"],
166	                    pos=(m["pos"][0], m["pos"][1]),
167	                    entity_type=m.get("type", "UNK"),
168	                ))
169	            entities.append(Entity(entity_id=eidx, mentions=mentions))
170	
171	        return document, sents, entities
172	
173	    def _find_hard_negatives(
174	        self, head: Entity, tail: Entity, evidence: list[int], n_sents: int
175	    ) -> list[int]:
176	        """找出 head/tail 共现但不在 gold evidence 中的句子作为 hard negatives。
177	
178	        优先选共现句（两个实体都出现的句子），不足时回退到单实体出现的句子。
179	        """
180	        evidence_set = set(evidence)
181	        head_sents = head.sent_ids
182	        tail_sents = tail.sent_ids
183	        cooccur = (head_sents & tail_sents) - evidence_set
184	        if cooccur:
185	            return sorted(cooccur)
186	        single_entity = (head_sents | tail_sents) - evidence_set
187	        return sorted(single_entity)
188	
189	    def process(self, split: str) -> list[RelationSample]:
190	        """处理指定 split，返回所有关系样本（含 hard negatives）。"""
191	        raw_docs = self.load_split(split)
192	        samples = []
193	        n_no_hard_neg = 0
194	
195	        for doc in raw_docs:
196	            doc_id = doc.get("title", "")
197	            document, sents, entities = self._parse_document(doc)
198	
199	            labels = doc.get("labels", [])
200	            for label in labels:
201	                h_idx, t_idx = label["h"], label["t"]
202	                relation = label["r"]
203	                evidence = label.get("evidence", [])
204	
205	                head = entities[h_idx]
206	                tail = entities[t_idx]
207	                hard_negs = self._find_hard_negatives(
208	                    head, tail, evidence, len(sents)
209	                )
210	                if not hard_negs:
211	                    n_no_hard_neg += 1
212	
213	                samples.append(RelationSample(
214	                    doc_id=doc_id,
215	                    document=document,
216	                    sents=sents,
217	                    head=head,
218	                    tail=tail,
219	                    relation=relation,
220	                    relation_name=DOCRED_REL_INFO.get(relation, relation),
221	                    evidence_sent_ids=evidence,
222	                    hard_negative_sent_ids=hard_negs,
223	                ))
224	
225	        logger.info(
226	            "Processed %d relation samples from %d documents "
227	            "(%d samples without hard negatives)",
228	            len(samples), len(raw_docs), n_no_hard_neg,
229	        )
230	        return samples
231	
232	    def group_by_document(
233	        self, samples: list[RelationSample]
234	    ) -> dict[str, list[RelationSample]]:
235	        """按文档 ID 分组，用于构建文档级训练样本。"""
236	        groups: dict[str, list[RelationSample]] = {}
237	        for s in samples:
238	            groups.setdefault(s.doc_id, []).append(s)
239	        return groups
240	
241	    def format_sft_sample(
242	        self, doc_samples: list[RelationSample]
243	    ) -> dict[str, str]:
244	        """将同一文档的所有关系样本格式化为 SFT 训练格式。
245	
246	        Returns:
247	            {"instruction": str, "input": str, "output": str}
248	        """
249	        if not doc_samples:
250	            return {"instruction": "", "input": "", "output": ""}
251	
252	        ref = doc_samples[0]
253	        numbered_sents = "\n".join(
254	            f"[{i}] {s}" for i, s in enumerate(ref.sents)
255	        )
256	
257	        entity_list = set()
258	        for s in doc_samples:
259	            entity_list.add(f"{s.head.name} ({s.head.mentions[0].entity_type})")
260	            entity_list.add(f"{s.tail.name} ({s.tail.mentions[0].entity_type})")
261	        entity_str = ", ".join(sorted(entity_list))
262	
263	        instruction = (
264	            "Extract all relation triples from the document. "
265	            "For each triple, provide the head entity, relation, tail entity, "
266	            "and the sentence IDs that serve as evidence. "
267	            "Output as a JSON list."
268	        )
269	
270	        input_text = (
271	            f"Document:\n{numbered_sents}\n\n"
272	            f"Entities: {entity_str}"
273	        )
274	
275	        triples = []
276	        for s in doc_samples:
277	            triples.append({
278	                "head": s.head.name,
279	                "relation": s.relation_name,
280	                "tail": s.tail.name,
281	                "evidence": s.evidence_sent_ids,
282	            })
283	        output_text = json.dumps(triples, ensure_ascii=False)
284	
285	        return {
286	            "instruction": instruction,
287	            "input": input_text,
288	            "output": output_text,
289	        }
290	
291	
292	def download_docred(output_dir: str) -> Path:
293	    """从 HuggingFace 下载 DocRED 数据集并保存为 JSON 文件。"""
294	    from datasets import load_dataset
295	
296	    out = Path(output_dir)
297	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
298	
299	    ds = load_dataset("thunlp/docred", trust_remote_code=True)
300	    for split_name, split_ds in ds.items():
301	        fname = SPLIT_FILENAMES.get(split_name, f"{split_name}.json")
302	        path = out / fname
303	        data = [row for row in split_ds]
304	        with open(path, "w") as f:
305	            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False)
306	        logger.info("Saved %d documents to %s", len(data), path)
307	
308	    return out
309	
310	
311	if __name__ == "__main__":
312	    import argparse
313	    import sys
314	
315	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s: %(message)s")
316	
317	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="DocRED 数据预处理")
318	    parser.add_argument("--data_dir", type=str, default=None,
319	                        help="DocRED JSON 文件目录，为空则从 HuggingFace 加载")
320	    parser.add_argument("--split", type=str, default="dev")
321	    parser.add_argument("--output", type=str, default=None,
322	                        help="输出处理后的样本到 JSON 文件")
323	    parser.add_argument("--download", action="store_true",
324	                        help="从 HuggingFace 下载 DocRED 到 --data_dir")
325	    args = parser.parse_args()
326	
327	    if args.download:
328	        if not args.data_dir:
329	            print("--download requires --data_dir", file=sys.stderr)
330	            sys.exit(1)
331	        download_docred(args.data_dir)
332	        sys.exit(0)
333	
334	    processor = DocREDProcessor(data_dir=args.data_dir)
335	    samples = processor.process(args.split)
336	
337	    print(f"Total samples: {len(samples)}")
338	    has_hard_neg = sum(1 for s in samples if s.hard_negative_sent_ids)
339	    print(f"Samples with hard negatives: {has_hard_neg} "
340	          f"({has_hard_neg / len(samples) * 100:.1f}%)")
341	
342	    if samples:
343	        s = samples[0]
344	        print(f"\nExample: {s.head.name} --[{s.relation_name}]--> {s.tail.name}")
345	        print(f"  Evidence sents: {s.evidence_sent_ids}")
346	        print(f"  Hard negatives: {s.hard_negative_sent_ids}")
347	
348	    if args.output:
349	        out_data = []
350	        for s in samples:
351	            out_data.append({
352	                "doc_id": s.doc_id,
353	                "head": s.head.name,
354	                "tail": s.tail.name,
355	                "relation": s.relation,
356	                "relation_name": s.relation_name,
357	                "evidence_sent_ids": s.evidence_sent_ids,
358	                "hard_negative_sent_ids": s.hard_negative_sent_ids,
359	            })
360	        with open(args.output, "w") as f:
361	            json.dump(out_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
362	        print(f"Saved to {args.output}")
363

1	"""MVP-1: CED 信号可行性离线验证。
2	
3	在 DocRED dev set 上对所有关系实例计算 CED margin，验证 DeBERTa NLI
4	模型能否区分 gold evidence 和 entity-cooccurrence distractors。
5	
6	Pass 条件:
7	  - mean(p_pos - p_neg) > 0.1
8	  - DeBERTa 判别准确率 > 70% (即 p_pos > p_neg 的比例)
9	  - Pearson r(CED margin, Jaccard diff) < 0.7（排除词汇重叠主导判别）
10	
11	输出: 汇总统计 + margin 分布直方图 + CED margin vs Jaccard diff scatter
12	"""
13	
14	import argparse
15	import json
16	import logging
17	import re
18	import sys
19	from pathlib import Path
20	
21	import numpy as np
22	from scipy import stats
23	
24	logging.basicConfig(
25	    level=logging.INFO,
26	    format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s",
27	)
28	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
29	
30	_TOKENIZE_RE = re.compile(r"\w+")
31	
32	
33	def _tokenize(text: str) -> set[str]:
34	    return set(_TOKENIZE_RE.findall(text.lower()))
35	
36	
37	def _token_jaccard(text_a: str, text_b: str) -> float:
38	    tokens_a = _tokenize(text_a)
39	    tokens_b = _tokenize(text_b)
40	    if not tokens_a or not tokens_b:
41	        return 0.0
42	    return len(tokens_a & tokens_b) / len(tokens_a | tokens_b)
43	
44	
45	def run_validation(
46	    data_dir: str = None,
47	    split: str = "dev",
48	    nli_model: str = "cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base",
49	    tau: float = 0.5,
50	    batch_size: int = 32,
51	    output_dir: str = ".",
52	    max_samples: int = 0,
53	):
54	    from freige.data.docred_processor import DocREDProcessor
55	    from freige.rewards.ced_reward import CEDRewardModel, verbalize_triple
56	
57	    processor = DocREDProcessor(data_dir=data_dir)
58	    samples = processor.process(split)
59	    logger.info("Total relation samples: %d", len(samples))
60	
61	    samples_with_negs = [s for s in samples if s.hard_negative_sent_ids]
62	    logger.info(
63	        "Samples with hard negatives: %d (%.1f%%)",
64	        len(samples_with_negs),
65	        len(samples_with_negs) / len(samples) * 100 if samples else 0,
66	    )
67	
68	    if max_samples > 0:
69	        samples_with_negs = samples_with_negs[:max_samples]
70	        logger.info("Using first %d samples", len(samples_with_negs))
71	
72	    reward_model = CEDRewardModel(model_name=nli_model)
73	
74	    claims = []
75	    evidence_lists = []
76	    negative_lists = []
77	
78	    for s in samples_with_negs:
79	        claim = verbalize_triple(s.head.name, s.relation, s.tail.name)
80	        evidence_sents = [s.sents[i] for i in s.evidence_sent_ids if i < len(s.sents)]
81	        negative_sents = [s.sents[i] for i in s.hard_negative_sent_ids if i < len(s.sents)]
82	        claims.append(claim)
83	        evidence_lists.append(evidence_sents)
84	        negative_lists.append(negative_sents)
85	
86	    logger.info("Computing CED rewards for %d samples...", len(claims))
87	
88	    all_results = []
89	    for start in range(0, len(claims), batch_size):
90	        end = min(start + batch_size, len(claims))
91	        batch_results = reward_model.compute_ced_reward_batch(
92	            claims[start:end],
93	            evidence_lists[start:end],
94	            negative_lists[start:end],
95	            tau=tau,
96	        )
97	        all_results.extend(batch_results)
98	        if (start // batch_size) % 10 == 0:
99	            logger.info("  Processed %d / %d", end, len(claims))
100	
101	    margins = np.array([r["margin"] for r in all_results])
102	    p_pos_arr = np.array([r["p_pos"] for r in all_results])
103	    p_neg_arr = np.array([r["p_neg"] for r in all_results])
104	    rewards = np.array([r["reward"] for r in all_results])
105	
106	    jaccard_pos_arr = np.zeros(len(claims))
107	    jaccard_neg_arr = np.zeros(len(claims))
108	    for i in range(len(claims)):
109	        evi_concat = " ".join(evidence_lists[i])
110	        jaccard_pos_arr[i] = _token_jaccard(claims[i], evi_concat)
111	        if negative_lists[i]:
112	            jaccard_neg_arr[i] = max(
113	                _token_jaccard(claims[i], neg) for neg in negative_lists[i]
114	            )
115	    jaccard_diff = jaccard_pos_arr - jaccard_neg_arr
116	
117	    pearson_r, pearson_p = stats.pearsonr(margins, jaccard_diff)
118	
119	    discrimination_acc = np.mean(margins > 0)
120	    mean_margin = np.mean(margins)
121	    median_margin = np.median(margins)
122	    std_margin = np.std(margins)
123	
124	    pass_margin = mean_margin > 0.1
125	    pass_acc = discrimination_acc > 0.70
126	    pass_lexical = abs(pearson_r) < 0.7
127	
128	    report_lines = [
129	        "=" * 60,
130	        "MVP-1: CED Signal Validation Report",
131	        "=" * 60,
132	        f"Split: {split}",
133	        f"Total relation samples: {len(samples)}",
134	        f"Samples with hard negatives: {len(samples_with_negs)}",
135	        f"NLI model: {nli_model}",
136	        f"Tau: {tau}",
137	        "",
138	        "--- p_pos (gold evidence → claim) ---",
139	        f"  Mean:   {np.mean(p_pos_arr):.4f}",
140	        f"  Median: {np.median(p_pos_arr):.4f}",
141	        f"  Std:    {np.std(p_pos_arr):.4f}",
142	        f"  > tau:  {np.mean(p_pos_arr > tau) * 100:.1f}%",
143	        "",
144	        "--- p_neg (hard negative → claim) ---",
145	        f"  Mean:   {np.mean(p_neg_arr):.4f}",
146	        f"  Median: {np.median(p_neg_arr):.4f}",
147	        f"  Std:    {np.std(p_neg_arr):.4f}",
148	        "",
149	        "--- CED Margin (p_pos - p_neg) ---",
150	        f"  Mean:   {mean_margin:.4f}  {'PASS' if pass_margin else 'FAIL'} (threshold: > 0.1)",
151	        f"  Median: {median_margin:.4f}",
152	        f"  Std:    {std_margin:.4f}",
153	        f"  Min:    {np.min(margins):.4f}",
154	        f"  Max:    {np.max(margins):.4f}",
155	        "",
156	        f"Discrimination accuracy (p_pos > p_neg): "
157	        f"{discrimination_acc * 100:.1f}%  {'PASS' if pass_acc else 'FAIL'} (threshold: > 70%)",
158	        "",
159	        "--- CED Reward ---",
160	        f"  Mean:   {np.mean(rewards):.4f}",
161	        f"  Non-zero: {np.mean(rewards > 0) * 100:.1f}%",
162	        "",
163	        "--- Lexical Overlap Diagnostics ---",
164	        f"  Token Jaccard (claim vs gold evidence):",
165	        f"    Mean:   {np.mean(jaccard_pos_arr):.4f}",
166	        f"    Median: {np.median(jaccard_pos_arr):.4f}",
167	        f"  Token Jaccard (claim vs hard negative):",
168	        f"    Mean:   {np.mean(jaccard_neg_arr):.4f}",
169	        f"    Median: {np.median(jaccard_neg_arr):.4f}",
170	        f"  Jaccard diff (pos - neg):",

172	        f"    Median: {np.median(jaccard_diff):.4f}",
173	        f"  Pearson r(CED margin, Jaccard diff): "
174	        f"{pearson_r:.4f} (p={pearson_p:.2e})  "
175	        f"{'PASS' if pass_lexical else 'FAIL'} (threshold: |r| < 0.7)",
176	        "",
177	        "=" * 60,
178	        f"MVP-1 VERDICT: {'PASS' if (pass_margin and pass_acc and pass_lexical) else 'FAIL'}",
179	        "=" * 60,
180	    ]
181	
182	    report = "\n".join(report_lines)
183	    print(report)
184	
185	    out = Path(output_dir)
186	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
187	
188	    report_path = out / "ced_signal_validation_report.txt"
189	    with open(report_path, "w") as f:
190	        f.write(report)
191	    logger.info("Report saved to %s", report_path)
192	
193	    detail_path = out / "ced_signal_validation_detail.json"
194	    detail_data = []
195	    for i, s in enumerate(samples_with_negs):
196	        detail_data.append({
197	            "doc_id": s.doc_id,
198	            "head": s.head.name,
199	            "tail": s.tail.name,
200	            "relation": s.relation,
201	            "relation_name": s.relation_name,

233	def _plot_figures(margins, p_pos, p_neg, jaccard_diff, pearson_r, output_dir: Path):
234	    """生成 margin 分布直方图 + lexical overlap scatter。"""
235	    try:
236	        import matplotlib
237	        matplotlib.use("Agg")
238	        import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
239	    except ImportError:
240	        logger.warning("matplotlib not available, skipping plots")
241	        return
242

244	
245	    axes[0, 0].hist(margins, bins=50, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.7, color="#4C72B0")
246	    axes[0, 0].axvline(x=0, color="red", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.5, label="margin=0")
247	    axes[0, 0].axvline(x=0.1, color="green", linestyle="--", linewidth=1.5, label="threshold=0.1")
248	    axes[0, 0].set_xlabel("CED Margin (p_pos - p_neg)")
249	    axes[0, 0].set_ylabel("Count")
250	    axes[0, 0].set_title("CED Margin Distribution")
251	    axes[0, 0].legend()
252	
253	    axes[0, 1].hist(p_pos, bins=50, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.7, color="#55A868", label="p_pos")
254	    axes[0, 1].hist(p_neg, bins=50, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.5, color="#C44E52", label="p_neg")
255	    axes[0, 1].set_xlabel("Entailment Probability")
256	    axes[0, 1].set_ylabel("Count")
257	    axes[0, 1].set_title("p_pos vs p_neg Distribution")
258	    axes[0, 1].legend()
259	
260	    axes[1, 0].scatter(p_neg, p_pos, alpha=0.15, s=8, color="#4C72B0")
261	    lims = [0, 1]
262	    axes[1, 0].plot(lims, lims, "r--", linewidth=1, label="y=x")
263	    axes[1, 0].set_xlabel("p_neg (hard negative)")
264	    axes[1, 0].set_ylabel("p_pos (gold evidence)")
265	    axes[1, 0].set_title("p_pos vs p_neg Scatter")
266	    axes[1, 0].set_xlim(lims)
267	    axes[1, 0].set_ylim(lims)
268	    axes[1, 0].set_aspect("equal")
269	    axes[1, 0].legend()
270	
271	    axes[1, 1].scatter(jaccard_diff, margins, alpha=0.15, s=8, color="#DD8452")
272	    z = np.polyfit(jaccard_diff, margins, 1)
273	    x_fit = np.linspace(jaccard_diff.min(), jaccard_diff.max(), 100)
274	    axes[1, 1].plot(x_fit, np.polyval(z, x_fit), "r-", linewidth=1.5,
275	                    label=f"r={pearson_r:.3f}")
276	    axes[1, 1].axhline(y=0, color="gray", linestyle=":", linewidth=1)
277	    axes[1, 1].set_xlabel("Jaccard Diff (pos - neg)")
278	    axes[1, 1].set_ylabel("CED Margin")
279	    axes[1, 1].set_title("CED Margin vs Lexical Overlap")
280	    axes[1, 1].legend()
281	
282	    plt.tight_layout()
283	    fig_path = output_dir / "ced_signal_validation.png"
284	    plt.savefig(fig_path, dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
285	    plt.close()

_source line 57 · 2026-05-15T12:33:28.644Z_

I have now gathered all the necessary information. Let me produce the detailed R1 review.

## R1 Review: Methods and Experiments

### Summary

FREIGE proposes CED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination), an NLI-based scoring function that contrasts gold evidence against entity-sharing distractors, and uses it as an offline quality signal for Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning (RSFT) to train LLMs to jointly produce relation triples and supporting evidence in document-level relation extraction. The paper includes a negative result on online RL (GRPO) and introduces EDCR as a diagnostic metric. The core pipeline is well-constructed and the negative GRPO result is genuinely informative, but several methodological concerns -- particularly around the EDCR definition, the fairness of the encoder-SOTA comparison, and incomplete statistical reporting -- need attention.

### Strengths

- **Well-motivated contrastive design.** The CED formulation (m = p_pos - p_neg, using entity-sharing sentences as hard negatives) directly targets a real failure mode of flat NLI scoring in DocRE: entity-mentioning sentences that do not support the specific relation still receive high entailment scores. The MVP-1 validation (discrimination accuracy 81.5%, Pearson r=0.34 against lexical overlap) provides convincing evidence that CED captures genuine semantic signal, not surface overlap.

- **Thorough GRPO failure analysis (Claim 3).** The paper systematically explores 3 reward types x 2 learning rates x multiple KL coefficients for GRPO+QLoRA, demonstrating consistent catastrophic forgetting. The decoupled observation that Evi-F1 *rises* to 0.850 while rel-F1 collapses to <0.02 (Table 6) is a novel and informative finding -- it shows CED reward *is* learnable but online RL destroys extraction capacity. The GRPO trajectory data across 6 checkpoints (Table 6) is detailed enough to be independently reproducible.

- **Clean ablation structure.** The CED vs flat-NLI RSFT ablation (Table 2) isolates the scoring function as the only variable. The dev set results (+2.8pp rel-F1, +2.6pp Evi-F1, -5.8pp EDCR for CED over flat-NLI) support the contrastive margin's superiority. Top-k (Table 9) and NLI model (Table 10) ablations address natural reviewer questions.

- **Multiple axes of generalization.** Model size ablation across Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B (Table 7) and cross-dataset evaluation on Re-DocRED (Table 8) strengthen the claim beyond a single-model, single-dataset contribution.

- **CED-as-reranker (Table 5).** Demonstrating that CED provides value at inference time (no extra training, Evi-F1=80.5, EDCR=49.8) with 8x inference cost is a practical contribution that broadens the paper's applicability.

- **Honest negative results.** The decisions log reveals the GRPO exploration was extensive and the pivot to RSFT was data-driven. The paper does not hide the failed RL experiments but frames them as a methodological contribution.

### Weaknesses

- **(Major) EDCR definition is problematic and potentially misleading.** The paper defines EDCR as the fraction of cited sentences that are *not* gold evidence. But the code (`evaluator.py`, lines 168-217) shows EDCR is computed over *all* predicted triples, not just correctly predicted ones. For incorrect triples, there is no gold evidence to compare against, meaning every cited sentence is automatically counted as a "distractor." This conflates two distinct failure modes: (1) citing wrong evidence for correct triples, and (2) predicting wrong triples (which trivially have 100% distractor rate). A model that predicts more triples (even if some are wrong) will mechanically have higher EDCR. The paper's claim that "EDCR captures evidence quality better than Evi-F1" (Claim 4, Table 3) is undermined if EDCR is dominated by the precision of relation extraction rather than evidence faithfulness. Suggestion: compute EDCR only on correctly-predicted triples, or decompose EDCR into matched-triple-EDCR vs unmatched-triple-EDCR.

- **(Major) Unfair comparison with encoder SOTA DREEAM.** Table 1 shows DREEAM rel-F1=63.3 vs RSFT+CED rel-F1=45.4 -- a gap of nearly 18pp. The paper correctly notes the evidence evaluation protocols differ (attention-based vs generative), but the headline claim of "first LLM-based DocRE with verifiable evidence" needs stronger framing around this massive extraction quality gap. More importantly, Evi-F1 comparison (80.2 vs 57.3) is potentially misleading because DREEAM's evidence is attention-based and not directly comparable. The paper should either (a) run DREEAM's predicted evidence through the same NLI-based evaluation, or (b) clearly caveat that Evi-F1 comparison across paradigms is not apple-to-apple.

- **(Major) No statistical significance testing.** Across all tables, results are reported as single-run point estimates. The paper mentions multiple seeds for RSFT (s42, s44, r2a, r2b) in the development log but only reports the single best configuration on test (Table 1). For Claim 2 (CED vs flat-NLI), the 2.8pp rel-F1 difference on dev could be within random variance. At minimum, the paper needs: (a) mean +/- std across 3 seeds for the main results, (b) a paired test (e.g., bootstrap or McNemar's) for the CED vs flat-NLI ablation.

- **(Major) RSFT combined scoring formula is underspecified.** In `rsft_score_filter.py` (line 212), the combined score is `format_reward * 0.2 + f1_reward * 0.4 + ced_reward * 0.4`. These weights are apparently not ablated anywhere in the paper. Since this scoring function is the selection criterion for RSFT training data, different weight allocations could significantly change results. This is a key design choice that receives no justification or ablation.

- **(Minor) Hard negative construction is limited.** Hard negatives are entity co-occurrence sentences not in gold evidence (`docred_processor.py`, lines 173-187). When no co-occurring sentence exists, single-entity sentences are used. For 44.4% of samples, there are no hard negatives at all (CED degenerates to flat NLI). The paper acknowledges this (MVP-1 report) but does not analyze how performance varies between samples with vs without hard negatives.

- **(Minor) Token truncation at 1024 affects 37% of outputs.** The memory log (D012) records that 37% of SFT outputs are truncated at max_new_tokens=1024, and the team ran a diagnostic (mvp2_eval_rsft_r2b_maxtok1536) to assess impact. The paper should report and discuss the truncation rate and its effect on metrics, especially since truncated JSON outputs are likely malformed and contribute to lower recall.

- **(Minor) CED tau=0.5 is fixed without sensitivity analysis.** The threshold tau in R_CED = max(0, p_pos - p_neg) * I(p_pos > tau) is set to 0.5 throughout. The MVP-1 analysis shows 33.4% of gold evidence has p_pos < 0.5, meaning CED reward is zero for a third of correct instances. A sweep over tau (0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6) would clarify sensitivity.

- **(Minor) Verbalization template dependency.** CED requires relation-specific natural language templates (`VERBALIZATION_TEMPLATES` in `ced_reward.py`, 96 relation types). The quality of these templates directly affects NLI judgments, but no analysis examines whether CED accuracy varies by relation type. Relations with poor verbalizations may systematically receive lower CED scores.

- **(Minor) EDCR-Evi-F1 correlation r=-0.42 is moderate.** The paper claims EDCR captures complementary information to Evi-F1, supported by Pearson r=-0.42 (Table 3). But with only 6-7 data points in the correlation (one per method), r=-0.42 is not statistically robust. This claim needs either more data points or a per-document correlation analysis.

### Questions for Authors

1. **EDCR on incorrect triples**: What fraction of EDCR is driven by incorrect triples (where every citation is trivially a "distractor")? If you recompute EDCR only on correctly-predicted triples, do the relative rankings between methods change?

2. **Combined scoring weights**: How sensitive is RSFT performance to the 0.2/0.4/0.4 weighting in the combined score? Have you tried f1-only selection, ced-only selection, or different weight ratios?

3. **Gold annotation incompleteness**: DocRED is known for annotation incompleteness (motivating Re-DocRED). Your CED treats all non-gold evidence sentences as potential distractors. Have you measured how often the "best distractor" (highest p_neg) is actually valid evidence missed by annotators? The paper_framing.md mentions a "self-correcting property" but no quantitative analysis is provided.

4. **Qwen3 generation_config override**: The decision log (D034/D036) reveals that Qwen3's built-in generation_config silently overrides temperature settings. Was this fixed *before* the final reported experiments? If the reported RSFT generations used the wrong temperature, the diversity of candidates is questionable.

5. **CED-as-reranker vs CED-RSFT inference cost**: CED-reranker requires 8x generation + NLI scoring at inference time. What is the wall-clock comparison? Is the RSFT model's single-pass inference substantially faster, and by how much?

6. **TaskEvalCallback bug**: The decision log documents a serious eval bug (D037/D038) where RSFT was falsely declared a failure due to mismatched eval set sizes. Has the evaluator code been corrected for all reported results? Are any numbers in the final paper affected by the pre-fix evaluation?

### Missing Experiments or Analysis

1. **EDCR decomposition**: Split EDCR into (a) distractor rate on matched triples and (b) distractor rate on unmatched triples. This disambiguates evidence quality from extraction quality.

2. **Combined score weight ablation**: At least 3 settings (ced-only, f1-only, equal weights) to demonstrate that the chosen 0.2/0.4/0.4 is not cherry-picked.

3. **Tau sensitivity**: A sweep of the CED threshold tau (0.3/0.4/0.5/0.6) with the RSFT pipeline.

4. **Per-relation-type CED analysis**: Do certain relation types systematically receive low CED scores due to verbalization quality? This would identify limitations of the template-based approach.

5. **Error analysis on GRPO collapse**: The paper claims GRPO failure is a fundamental limitation of online RL for structured IE, but only tests GRPO+QLoRA. A single full-precision GRPO run (even abbreviated) would disambiguate whether QLoRA quantization is a confound. The decision log (D008) explicitly identifies "QLoRA 4-bit quantization noise" as a root cause for KL explosion, undermining the claim that online RL itself is the problem.

6. **Statistical significance**: Bootstrap confidence intervals for the main results, paired significance tests for CED vs flat-NLI.

7. **Human evaluation of evidence quality**: Even a small-scale (50-100 triples) human annotation of whether cited evidence actually supports the predicted relation would validate that Evi-F1 and EDCR reflect genuine evidence quality.

### Preliminary Scores

- **Soundness: 3/5** -- The core CED+RSFT pipeline is sound, but the EDCR metric has a definition flaw (computed over all triples, not just correct ones), the GRPO failure analysis conflates QLoRA quantization effects with online RL limitations, and there is no statistical significance testing.

- **Excitement: 3/5** -- The problem (evidence for LLM-based DocRE) is well-motivated and practically relevant. The CED scoring idea is neat. However, the 18pp extraction gap to encoder SOTA limits the practical impact, and the negative GRPO result, while informative, is hard to generalize given the QLoRA confound. The contribution is solid but incremental.

- **Overall: 3/5** -- Borderline accept. The paper addresses a genuine gap (no LLM-based DocRE system produces evidence), proposes a reasonable solution (CED+RSFT), and includes informative negative results (GRPO failure). However, the EDCR definition issue is a threat to Claim 4, the lack of statistical testing weakens Claims 1-2, and the GRPO analysis needs disambiguation from QLoRA effects. With the EDCR fix and significance testing, this would be a solid 3.5-4.

- **Confidence: 4/5** -- I have read the full codebase, experiment logs, and design decisions. My concerns about EDCR, statistical testing, and the QLoRA confound are well-grounded in the evidence. I am less confident about the practical significance threshold for the CED vs flat-NLI improvement, which would benefit from seeing variance across seeds.
